```text
    1.5.4 - GUI fit at 800x600 (QGIS Dev's review, 2026-11-03): the Settings window (needed 841 px of height) and Build offline pack (645 px) scroll their body, with the Save / Close and Build / Stop / Close buttons kept under it. Shared damsuite: servers.py names the 3DHP flowlines service. No default changes.
    1.5.3 - USGS flowlines come from the 3DHP service (3DHP_all/MapServer/50) as the nd Hazard Helper, constants.py shared with DamSafety_Hazard 2.22.6 (audit 2026-11-03, fix D; Nick: "3DHP").
    1.5.2 - Offline pack: the format-1 pack holds the NLCD land cover too (nd_offline_pack/landcover/NLCD_2021_landcover.gpkg, the format-2 file copied, listed in its manifest "rasters"); --zip1 ZIP writes the format-1 pack as one zip (sqlite, manifest, README, rasters); --verify checks both packs list the same rasters. Shared with DamSafety Hazard 2.20.3: the HIFLD lifelines of the pack are read; offline_pack unpacks a zipped format-1 pack's rasters.
    1.5.1 - Offline pack: NHD_flowlines / NHD_waterbodies are read from 3DHP area by area (0.25 degree tiles; a tile that times out is split) and only the areas 3DHP cannot serve are filled from NHD - the 3DHP features already read are kept (1.5.0 threw them away and used NHD for the whole layer). Every hydro feature has hydro_src (3DHP / NHD); the manifest gives the counts per source and the areas filled or not read, and fallback_reason says why. HIFLD files are credited by what is known (the reference pack's files by sha256: hsdl.org for fire / EOC / law, DataLumos with a flagged project number for wastewater; any other file neutrally). DWR / HIFLD file dates are the file's own. Shared pack engine 1.3 (Hazard 2.20.2).
    1.5.0 - Build offline pack… (Data menu) downloads every layer of the ND dam suite's offline pack (structures, roads, railroads, lifelines, hydro, DWR dams / dam lines / dugouts from the DWR MapService, ND_counties, the four HIFLD layers, the ND DEQ layers, NLCD 2021 land cover) into a folder you choose and writes it in both formats from the same download: a new ND_Dam_Suite_Pack (one GeoPackage per layer, format 2) and nd_offline_pack/nd_offline_pack.sqlite (every vector layer, format 1; the raster is format 2 only and named in its manifest). All pack layers or a chosen few; stopped builds resume and finished layers are kept. Both manifests give each layer's source URL, date, count and sha256. An existing pack (or the one the plugins read) is never written into. DWR_unknown_structures needs a local DWR file (not published online). No default changes. PR #221 sources: HIFLD layers from the Data Rescue Project's DataLumos copies (a folder of downloaded files, clipped to ND; left out without one; no mirror fallback; wastewater = DataLumos 239262); HPMS_ND_2024 kept next to NDDOT_traffic_counts for AADT comparison (noted in both manifests and LAYERS.txt); ND DEQ landfills and community / non-community source water protection areas (NDDEQ_*, nddeq/ folder, from the ND GIS Hub); 3DHP replaces NHD under the NHD_flowlines / NHD_waterbodies names with NHD fields added (NHD service only as fallback, named in the manifest); new SARP Aquatic Barrier Inventory layers ABI_dams, ABI_small_barriers_surveyed, ABI_road_crossings_unsurveyed (barriers/ folder, network field on/off-network, removed / excluded barriers kept and flagged in barrier_status, citation and terms in the manifests). Download imagery for these dams… (Data menu): NAIP from the USGS National Map NAIP ImageServer around each dam (all, selected, or listed ids; 2.5 km radius by default; 0.6, 1 or 2 m), one JPEG YCbCr COG per dam in <output>/imagery/<dam_id>_naip_<year>.tif with a manifest (URL, year, resolution, sha256); size shown first; resumable; never written into a pack.
    1.4.4 - GUI grammar, part 2: Save and Cancel carry no icons (the suite's buttons are text only). No default changes.
    1.4.3 - GUI grammar, part 1: window title 'DamSafety Data 1.4.3' (was 'ND Data Updater'); ui_fit.py and tokens.py are the suite's shared copies (shared/ui/). No default changes.
    1.4.2 - PR #94 (Claude's audit-fix campaign): H14 Qt6 scoped-enum crashes fixed (qtcompat.py); M21, M22, M23, L3, P1: NSI generator hash, source fixes and the pid-lock rule (a lock never steals a live same-process lock). No default changes.
    1.4.1 - The Settings window is sized for the screen QGIS is on (else the screen under the mouse), not always the primary screen; it opens inside that screen and never larger than it, and the size cap follows it to another monitor (ui_fit.py, the same file as the Hazard's and Screening's).
    1.4.0 - Uses the suite data folder (audit 1.22 item 7): when ndsuite/data_folder is set, downloads go to <suite folder>/structures/. A folder inside the offline pack (ND_Dam_Suite_Pack) or one that can't be written is skipped with a message. Downloads already in ~/Documents/ND Structure Data stay in use, with a notice on how to move them, until structures/ holds data; nothing is moved automatically. A Data folder you choose in Settings still wins. Data's manifest is now structures_manifest.json, so it never replaces the suite's manifest.json (the old manifest.json is read once).
    1.3.6 - Cross-platform (#32): UTF-8 text I/O and CSVs written with newline=''.
    1.3.2 - Added ND 911 Site/Structure Address Points (NENA) from the ND GIS Hub FeatureServer. Downloads as address_points (GeoPackage if ogr2ogr available, otherwise NDJSON). Also added to the offline pack builder so the suite pack includes the layer. suite_fill loads address_points with structures for Hazard verification.
  1.3.1 - Overture source now downloads and converts buildings for the ND bounding box using the overturemaps CLI (pip install overturemaps). Output is overture_buildings.gpkg (or .geojson). suite_fill recognizes the layer name.
  1.3.0 - Added Overture Maps Buildings as an optional source. Layer name overture_buildings is recognized by suite_fill alongside NSI and USA Structures. pack_sources.py documents accepted structure layer names so Screening/Runner/Hazard correctly use pack layers.
  1.2.1 - OSM source now uses headers mode (ETag / Last-Modified) instead of MD5 sidecar. Geofabrik does not publish an MD5 for the .gpkg.zip.
  1.2.0 - Added Build ND Offline Pack action. Pack can include 3DHP flowlines and waterbodies (in addition to NHD) and three Aquatic Barriers layers (dams, surveyed crossings, unsurveyed crossings) for North Dakota. 3DHP is preferred hydrography; NHD retained. Aquatic Barriers from Great Plains tool (private records excluded).
  1.1.0 - NSI now names its version (the API default switched to NSI 2026). By default NSI 2026 and NSI 2022 are merged into one layer: a 2022 point within 30 m of a 2026 point is folded into it (2026 wins; the 2022 id, occupancy, building type and a mobile-home flag are kept in nsi22_* fields), and 2022 points with no 2026 point nearby are kept with nsi_version = 2022. Settings choose merged / 2026 only / 2022 only and the match distance. nsi_summary.json in the nsi folder gives counts per county.
  1.0.1 - OpenStreetMap now downloads as a GeoPackage (north-dakota-latest-free.gpkg.zip); only its buildings layer is added to the project.
  1.0.0 - First release.
```

## Archived metadata changelog — DamSafety_Setup

Entries moved verbatim out of `DamSafety_Setup/metadata.txt` `changelog=` on 2026-11-07 (QGIS's Plugin Manager parses and renders the whole `changelog=` value of every installed plugin, so `tools/ci_checks.py` now caps it at 12 KB and keeps the newest entries there). Newest first; the indentation is the metadata.txt continuation indent.

```text
    1.0.13 - GUI grammar, part 1: plugin icon; wizard title 'DamSafety Setup — Set up one dam'; Browse… buttons; colours from the shared tokens.py. No default changes.
    1.0.11 - PR #94 (Claude's audit-fix campaign): M24 the Setup status panel is wired and released on unload; M4 pack_convert fixes. No default changes.
    1.0.10 - Dam page: (1) NID fill: a height or storage the DWR record does not have (blank or 0) is filled from the National Inventory of Dams - the NID layer in the suite data folder at once, the NID web service only when "Fill from NID (online)" is clicked; a DWR value is never replaced (a storage still blank takes the DWR normal pool volume first). (2) Records that are not a dam to model (status Removed / Breached / Not Built / Submerged, remarks 'VOID ...', 'No Dam Visible') are hidden unless "Show all records" is ticked, each with its reason. (3) "Estimate from DEM": screening height (crest along the dam line minus the lowest ground just downstream) and storage (DEM volume below the crest in the closed basin upstream), in a background task, only into blank values. The source shows next to each value (from DWR / from NID ND00189 / estimated from DEM (screening only) / typed in) and goes to the Runner hand-off. A dam with no point takes its dam line midpoint (EPSG:4326). Max storage goes up to 50 million ac-ft.
    1.0.9 - One ellipsis character (U+2026) on the plugin menu entry (Start here…).
    1.0.8 - Wizard live checks: Next stays off until the page is valid (folder holds the DWR dams; a dam with height and storage above 0 - no more 0 ft / 0 ac-ft hand-over; a readable DEM) and the reason shows in red under the control. Folder scan as coloured marks, the number of matching dams, where the downloaded DEM is saved, and a Run summary that states the grid / run length / EAP values the Runner gets (Advanced values apply also when its box is unticked, as before).
    1.0.7 - Cross-platform (#32): explicit UTF-8 text I/O in the pack converter.
```
